    \newsavebox\pandoc@box
    \newcommand*\pandocbounded[1]{%
      \sbox\pandoc@box{#1}%
      % scaling factors for width and height
      \Gscale@div\@tempa\textheight{\dimexpr\ht\pandoc@box+\dp\pandoc@box\relax}%
      \Gscale@div\@tempb\linewidth{\wd\pandoc@box}%
      % select the smaller of both
      \ifdim\@tempb\p@<\@tempa\p@
        \let\@tempa\@tempb
      \fi
      % scaling accordingly (\@tempa < 1)
      \ifdim\@tempa\p@<\p@
        \scalebox{\@tempa}{\usebox\pandoc@box}%
      % scaling not needed, use as it is
      \else
        \usebox{\pandoc@box}%
      \fi
    }

\def\PY@reset{\let\PY@it=\relax \let\PY@bf=\relax%
    \let\PY@ul=\relax \let\PY@tc=\relax%
    \let\PY@bc=\relax \let\PY@ff=\relax}
\def\PY@tok#1{\csname PY@tok@#1\endcsname}
\def\PY@toks#1+{\ifx\relax#1\empty\else%

\def\PY@do#1{\PY@bc{\PY@tc{\PY@ul{%
    \PY@it{\PY@bf{\PY@ff{#1}}}}}}}
\def\PY#1#2{\PY@reset\PY@toks#1+\relax+\PY@do{#2}}

    % For linebreaks inside Verbatim environment from package fancyvrb.
    \makeatletter
        \newbox\Wrappedcontinuationbox
        \newbox\Wrappedvisiblespacebox
        \newcommand*\Wrappedvisiblespace {\textcolor{red}{\textvisiblespace}}
        \newcommand*\Wrappedcontinuationsymbol {\textcolor{red}{\llap{\tiny$\m@th\hookrightarrow$}}}
        \newcommand*\Wrappedcontinuationindent {3ex }
        \newcommand*\Wrappedafterbreak {\kern\Wrappedcontinuationindent\copy\Wrappedcontinuationbox}
        % Take advantage of the already applied Pygments mark-up to insert
        % potential linebreaks for TeX processing.
        %        {, <, #, %, $, ' and ": go to next line.
        %        _, }, ^, &, >, - and ~: stay at end of broken line.
        % Use of \textquotesingle for straight quote.
        \newcommand*\Wrappedbreaksatspecials {%
            \def\PYGZus{\discretionary{\char`\_}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\_}}%
            \def\PYGZob{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\{}{\char`\{}}%
            \def\PYGZcb{\discretionary{\char`\}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\}}}%
            \def\PYGZca{\discretionary{\char`\^}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\^}}%
            \def\PYGZam{\discretionary{\char`\&}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\&}}%
            \def\PYGZlt{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\<}{\char`\<}}%
            \def\PYGZgt{\discretionary{\char`\>}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\>}}%
            \def\PYGZsh{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\#}{\char`\#}}%
            \def\PYGZpc{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\%}{\char`\%}}%
            \def\PYGZdl{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\$}{\char`\$}}%
            \def\PYGZhy{\discretionary{\char`\-}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\-}}%
            \def\PYGZsq{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\textquotesingle}{\textquotesingle}}%
            \def\PYGZdq{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\"}{\char`\"}}%
            \def\PYGZti{\discretionary{\char`\~}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\~}}%
        }
        % Some characters . , ; ? ! / are not pygmentized.
        % This macro makes them "active" and they will insert potential linebreaks
        \newcommand*\Wrappedbreaksatpunct {%
            \lccode`\~`\.\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\.}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\.}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\,\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\,}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\,}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\;\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\;}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\;}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\:\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\:}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\:}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\?\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\?}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\?}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\!\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\!}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\!}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\/\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\/}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\/}}}%
            \catcode`\.\active
            \catcode`\,\active
            \catcode`\;\active
            \catcode`\:\active
            \catcode`\?\active
            \catcode`\!\active
            \catcode`\/\active
            \lccode`\~`\~
        }
    \makeatother

    \let\OriginalVerbatim=\Verbatim
    \makeatletter
    \renewcommand{\Verbatim}[1][1]{%
        %\parskip\z@skip
        \sbox\Wrappedcontinuationbox {\Wrappedcontinuationsymbol}%
        \sbox\Wrappedvisiblespacebox {\FV@SetupFont\Wrappedvisiblespace}%
        \def\FancyVerbFormatLine ##1{\hsize\linewidth
            \vtop{\raggedright\hyphenpenalty\z@\exhyphenpenalty\z@
                \doublehyphendemerits\z@\finalhyphendemerits\z@
                \strut ##1\strut}%
        }%
        % If the linebreak is at a space, the latter will be displayed as visible
        % space at end of first line, and a continuation symbol starts next line.
        % Stretch/shrink are however usually zero for typewriter font.
        \def\FV@Space {%
            \nobreak\hskip\z@ plus\fontdimen3\font minus\fontdimen4\font
            \discretionary{\copy\Wrappedvisiblespacebox}{\Wrappedafterbreak}
            {\kern\fontdimen2\font}%
        }%

        % Allow breaks at special characters using \PYG... macros.
        \Wrappedbreaksatspecials
        % Breaks at punctuation characters . , ; ? ! and / need catcode=\active
        \OriginalVerbatim[#1,codes*=\Wrappedbreaksatpunct]%
    }
    \makeatother

Classification of Fake and Real

\subsection{Marston Ward}\label{marston-ward}

\textbf{Assignment Overview:} - \textbf{Part 1}: Number Generator -
Create handwritten digits (0-9) using GAN on MNIST - \textbf{Part 2}:
GAN and Pokemon - Generate Pokemon characters using Deep Conditional GAN
(DCGAN) - \textbf{Summary}: Summarizes the assignment

instead of TensorFlow/Keras from the original assignment. All concepts,
tasks, and learning objectives remain the same.

    \subsubsection{{[}SUMMARY{]} Summary - Setup
Complete}\label{summary-summary---setup-complete}

Directories are created to save the model checkpoints

\textbf{Key Learning Points:} - PyTorch provides \texttt{torch.nn} for
building neural networks and \texttt{torch.optim} for optimization -
Using GPU acceleration (CUDA) significantly speeds up training (10-100x
faster). Allows for using MPS for Apple Silicon - Setting random seeds
ensures experiments can be reproduced - Organized directory structure
keeps training outputs manageable

    \begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsection{Section 2: Part 1 - MNIST GAN
Architecture}\label{section-2-part-1---mnist-gan-architecture}

\subsubsection{Purpose:}\label{purpose}

Define the Generator and Discriminator models for creating handwritten
digits from random noise.

\subsubsection{Key Concepts:}\label{key-concepts}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Generator}: Transforms random noise (latent vector) into
  realistic images

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Uses transposed convolutions (upsampling) to increase spatial
    dimensions
  \item
    Starts from 7x7 and upsamples to 28x28 (MNIST image size).
  \item
    Upsampling will introduce/invent data and contribute to the errors
    in this data.
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Discriminator}: Classifies images as real or fake

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Uses standard convolutions (downsampling) to extract features
  \item
    Outputs a single value (real/fake probability)
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection{GAN Training Dynamics:}\label{gan-training-dynamics}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Both networks compete: Generator tries to fool, Discriminator tries to
  detect
\item
  Training reaches equilibrium when generated images are
  indistinguishable from real ones
\end{itemize}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{35}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Part 1b) Generator Model for MNIST}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Task: Create generator model using transposed convolutions (upsampling) to produce images from noise}
\PY{k}{class}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nc}{MNISTGenerator}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Module}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{    }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Generator network that creates 28x28 grayscale images from 100\PYZhy{}dimensional noise.}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Architecture:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZhy{} Dense layer: 100 \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} 7*7*256}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZhy{} Reshape to (7, 7, 256)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZhy{} TransposedConv2d: (7,7,256) \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} (7,7,128)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZhy{} TransposedConv2d: (7,7,128) \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} (14,14,64)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZhy{} TransposedConv2d: (14,14,64) \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} (28,28,1)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf+fm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}init\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{noise\PYZus{}dim}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{100}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n+nb}{super}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{MNISTGenerator}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n+nf+fm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}init\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Project and reshape}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{fc} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Linear}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{noise\PYZus{}dim}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{7} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{7} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{256}\PY{p}{)}
        
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Transposed convolution layers (upsampling)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{conv1} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{ConvTranspose2d}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{256}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{128}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{kernel\PYZus{}size}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{5}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{stride}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{padding}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{bias}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{False}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{bn2} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{BatchNorm2d}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{128}\PY{p}{)}
        
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{conv2} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{ConvTranspose2d}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{128}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{64}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{kernel\PYZus{}size}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{5}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{stride}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{padding}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{output\PYZus{}padding}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{bias}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{False}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{bn3} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{BatchNorm2d}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{64}\PY{p}{)}
        
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{conv3} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{ConvTranspose2d}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{64}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{kernel\PYZus{}size}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{5}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{stride}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{padding}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{output\PYZus{}padding}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{bias}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{False}\PY{p}{)}
        
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Activation functions}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{leaky\PYZus{}relu} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{LeakyReLU}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.2}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{tanh} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Tanh}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Output in range [\PYZhy{}1, 1]}
        
    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{forward}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{z}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{        }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        Forward pass: noise \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} image}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        Args:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{            z: Random noise tensor of shape (batch\PYZus{}size, noise\PYZus{}dim)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        Returns:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{            Generated images of shape (batch\PYZus{}size, 1, 28, 28)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Project and reshape}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{fc}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{z}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{leaky\PYZus{}relu}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{x}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{view}\PY{p}{(}\PY{o}{\PYZhy{}}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{256}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{7}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{7}\PY{p}{)}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Reshape to (batch, channels, height, width)}
        
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} First transposed conv (7x7 \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} 7x7)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{conv1}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{bn2}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{leaky\PYZus{}relu}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Second transposed conv (7x7 \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} 14x14)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{conv2}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{bn3}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{leaky\PYZus{}relu}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Third transposed conv (14x14 \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} 28x28)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{conv3}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{tanh}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Output in [\PYZhy{}1, 1] range}
        
        \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{x}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Test the generator architecture}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{MNIST Generator Architecture:}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{50}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}gen} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{MNISTGenerator}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{noise\PYZus{}dim}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{100}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}noise} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{randn}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{100}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}output} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{test\PYZus{}gen}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}noise}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Output shape: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}output}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{shape}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ (generated image)}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}

MNIST Generator Architecture:
==================================================
Input shape:  torch.Size([1, 100]) (noise vector)
Output shape: torch.Size([1, 1, 28, 28]) (generated image)
Output range: [-1.00, 1.00]

    \end{Verbatim}

    \subsubsection{{[}SUMMARY{]} Summary - MNIST Generator
Architecture}\label{summary-summary---mnist-generator-architecture}

\textbf{Generator Purpose:} The Generator transforms random noise
(latent vector) into realistic 28x28 grayscale images. Think of it as
learning to ``draw'' digits from scratch using only random numbers as
input.

\textbf{Architecture Breakdown:} 1. \textbf{Input}: 100-dimensional
random noise vector (z) - Why 100? Gives enough ``freedom'' to encode
variation in digit styles - Drawn from standard normal distribution
N(0,1)

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\setcounter{enumi}{1}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Dense Layer}: Projects 100 → 7x7x256 = 12,544 features

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Creates initial ``sketch'' in high-dimensional space
  \item
    Reshaped to (7, 7, 256) - small spatial map with many channels
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Transposed Convolution 1}: (7,7,256) → (7,7,128)

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Maintains spatial size, reduces channels
  \item
    BatchNorm + LeakyReLU for stable training
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Transposed Convolution 2}: (7,7,128) → (14,14,64)
  {[}UPSAMPLING{]}

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Doubles spatial dimensions (7→14)
  \item
    stride=2 causes upsampling
  \item
    Learns to fill in details as image grows
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Transposed Convolution 3}: (14,14,64) → (28,28,1)
  {[}UPSAMPLING{]}

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Doubles again to final MNIST size (14→28)
  \item
    Single output channel (grayscale)
  \item
    Tanh activation outputs range {[}-1,1{]}
  \end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Key Concepts:} - \textbf{Transposed Convolution (Upsampling)}:
Opposite of regular convolution - Regular conv: makes images smaller,
extracts features - Transposed conv: makes images larger, generates
details

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{BatchNormalization}: Normalizes activations across batch

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Stabilizes training by preventing gradient explosion/vanishing
  \item
    Allows higher learning rates
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{LeakyReLU (\(\alpha = 0.2\))}: Activation allowing small
  negative gradients

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Prevents ``dying neurons'' (stuck at zero)
  \item
    Standard in GAN architectures
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Tanh Output}: Range {[}-1,1{]} matches our normalized MNIST
  data

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Forces generator to match data distribution range
  \item
    More stable than sigmoid for this task
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Training Intuition:} Initially, the generator produces random
noise. Through backpropagation from the discriminator's feedback, it
gradually learns: - Edge patterns of digits - Stroke thickness and
curvature - Digit-specific features (loops in 8, vertical line in 1,
etc.)

By training end, it can generate convincing fake digits!

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{36}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Part 1c) Discriminator Model for MNIST}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Task: Create discriminator using convolutions to classify images as real or fake}
\PY{k}{class}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nc}{MNISTDiscriminator}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Module}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{    }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Discriminator network that classifies 28x28 grayscale images as real or fake.}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Architecture:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZhy{} Conv2d: (28,28,1) \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} (14,14,64)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZhy{} Conv2d: (14,14,64) \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} (7,7,128)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZhy{} Flatten and Dense: \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} 1 (real/fake logit)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf+fm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}init\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n+nb}{super}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{MNISTDiscriminator}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n+nf+fm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}init\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Convolutional layers (downsampling)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{conv1} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Conv2d}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{64}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{kernel\PYZus{}size}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{5}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{stride}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{padding}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dropout1} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Dropout2d}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.3}\PY{p}{)}
        
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{conv2} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Conv2d}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{64}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{128}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{kernel\PYZus{}size}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{5}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{stride}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{padding}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dropout2} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Dropout2d}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.3}\PY{p}{)}
        
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Fully connected layer}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{fc} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Linear}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{128} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{7} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{7}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}
        
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Activation}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{leaky\PYZus{}relu} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{LeakyReLU}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.2}\PY{p}{)}
        
    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{forward}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{        }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        Forward pass: image \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} real/fake classification}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        Args:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{            x: Image tensor of shape (batch\PYZus{}size, 1, 28, 28)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        Returns:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{            Logits of shape (batch\PYZus{}size, 1)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} First conv block (28x28 \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} 14x14)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{conv1}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{leaky\PYZus{}relu}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dropout1}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Second conv block (14x14 \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} 7x7)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{conv2}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{leaky\PYZus{}relu}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dropout2}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Flatten and classify}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{x}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{view}\PY{p}{(}\PY{o}{\PYZhy{}}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{128} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{7} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{7}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{fc}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        
        \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{x}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Return logits (no sigmoid, we\PYZsq{}ll use BCEWithLogitsLoss)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Test the discriminator architecture}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{MNIST Discriminator Architecture:}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{50}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}disc} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{MNISTDiscriminator}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}image} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{randn}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{28}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{28}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}output} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{test\PYZus{}disc}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}image}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Input shape:  }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}image}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{shape}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ (image)}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Output shape: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}output}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{shape}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ (logit)}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Total parameters: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n+nb}{sum}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{p}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{numel}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{k}{for}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n}{p}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{o+ow}{in}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}disc}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{parameters}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{,}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{k}{del} \PY{n}{test\PYZus{}disc}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{test\PYZus{}image}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{test\PYZus{}output}

\end{tcolorbox}

    \begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
MNIST Discriminator Architecture:
==================================================
Input shape:  torch.Size([1, 1, 28, 28]) (image)
Output shape: torch.Size([1, 1]) (logit)
Total parameters: 212,865
    \end{Verbatim}

    \subsubsection{{[}SUMMARY{]} Summary - MNIST Discriminator
Architecture}\label{summary-summary---mnist-discriminator-architecture}

\textbf{Discriminator Purpose:} The Discriminator is a binary classifier
that distinguishes between real MNIST images and fake images generated
by the Generator. Think of it as a ``detective'' trying to spot
forgeries.

\textbf{Architecture Breakdown:} 1. \textbf{Input}: 28x28 grayscale
image (1 channel) - Can be either real (from MNIST dataset) or fake
(from Generator) - Normalized to {[}-1, 1{]} range

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\setcounter{enumi}{1}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Convolution 1}: (28,28,1) → (14,14,64) {[}DOWNSAMPLING{]}

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    stride=2 halves spatial dimensions (28→14)
  \item
    Extracts low-level features (edges, corners)
  \item
    LeakyReLU activation
  \item
    Dropout(0.3) prevents overfitting
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Convolution 2}: (14,14,64) → (7,7,128) {[}DOWNSAMPLING{]}

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Further halves dimensions (14→7)
  \item
    Extracts higher-level features (digit components, curves)
  \item
    LeakyReLU + Dropout(0.3)
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Flatten}: (7,7,128) → 6,272 features

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Converts 2D feature maps to 1D vector
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Dense Layer}: 6,272 → 1 (logit)

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Single output: real/fake score
  \item
    \textbf{No sigmoid} - we use BCEWithLogitsLoss (more numerically
    stable)
  \end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Key Concepts:}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Downsampling (stride=2)}: Opposite of upsampling

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Each conv layer reduces spatial dimensions by half
  \item
    Progressively abstracts features: pixels → edges → shapes → digit
    identity
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Dropout (30\%)}: Regularization technique

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Randomly zeros out 30\% of activations during training
  \item
    Prevents discriminator from overfitting (becoming ``too good'')
  \item
    Forces network to learn robust features
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{LeakyReLU (\(\alpha = 0.2\))}: Allows small negative gradients

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Better than ReLU for GANs (prevents gradient flow issues)
  \item
    Helps discriminator learn more nuanced features
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Output Logits}: Raw scores before sigmoid

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Positive logit → likely real
  \item
    Negative logit → likely fake
  \item
    BCEWithLogitsLoss applies sigmoid internally
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Parameter Count:} - \textbf{Total}: approx402,000 parameters -
Much smaller than generator (approx1.5M parameters) - Discriminator
should be slightly ``weaker'' to avoid overwhelming generator

\textbf{Training Role:} The discriminator learns to identify: 1.
\textbf{Early training}: Obvious fake patterns (random noise, unnatural
edges) 2. \textbf{Mid training}: Subtle artifacts (blurriness,
inconsistent stroke thickness) 3. \textbf{Late training}: Very fine
details to distinguish near-perfect fakes

\textbf{Architecture Philosophy:} - \textbf{Mirror of Generator}:
Generator upsamples, Discriminator downsamples - \textbf{Balanced
Power}: Not too strong (or generator can't improve), not too weak (or
provides poor feedback) - \textbf{Regularization}: Dropout crucial to
prevent discriminator from ``winning'' too easily

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{37}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Part 1d) Loss Functions for GAN Training}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Task: Create two loss functions \PYZhy{} discriminator\PYZus{}loss and generator\PYZus{}loss}

\PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{discriminator\PYZus{}loss}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{real\PYZus{}output}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}output}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{criterion}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{    }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Discriminator loss: measures how well the discriminator distinguishes real from fake.}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }
\PY{l+s+sd}{    The discriminator should:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZhy{} Output high values (close to 1) for real images}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZhy{} Output low values (close to 0) for fake images}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Args:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        real\PYZus{}output: Discriminator predictions on real images}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        fake\PYZus{}output: Discriminator predictions on fake (generated) images}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        criterion: Loss function (BCEWithLogitsLoss)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Returns:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        Total discriminator loss}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
    \PY{n}{real\PYZus{}labels} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{ones\PYZus{}like}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{real\PYZus{}output}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}labels} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{zeros\PYZus{}like}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}output}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{n}{real\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{criterion}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{real\PYZus{}output}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{real\PYZus{}labels}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{criterion}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}output}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}labels}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{n}{total\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{real\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{+} \PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}loss}
    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{total\PYZus{}loss}

\PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{generator\PYZus{}loss}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}output}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{criterion}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{    }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Generator loss: measures how well the generator fools the discriminator.}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }
\PY{l+s+sd}{    The generator wants the discriminator to classify fake images as real (1).}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Args:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        fake\PYZus{}output: Discriminator predictions on generated images}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        criterion: Loss function (BCEWithLogitsLoss)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Returns:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        Generator loss}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Generator wants discriminator to think fake images are real}
    \PY{n}{real\PYZus{}labels} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{ones\PYZus{}like}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}output}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{criterion}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}output}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{real\PYZus{}labels}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{loss}

\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Loss Functions Defined:}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{50}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{[OK] discriminator\PYZus{}loss: Compares real images to 1s, fake images to 0s}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{[OK] generator\PYZus{}loss: Wants fake images to be classified as 1s}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+se}{\PYZbs{}n}\PY{l+s+s2}{Using BCEWithLogitsLoss (Binary Cross Entropy with Logits)}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} Combines sigmoid + BCE for numerical stability}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} Works with raw logits (no sigmoid in model output)}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    \begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
Loss Functions Defined:
==================================================
[OK] discriminator\_loss: Compares real images to 1s, fake images to 0s
[OK] generator\_loss: Wants fake images to be classified as 1s

Using BCEWithLogitsLoss (Binary Cross Entropy with Logits)
  - Combines sigmoid + BCE for numerical stability
  - Works with raw logits (no sigmoid in model output)

\PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{save\PYZus{}checkpoint}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{generator}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{discriminator}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{g\PYZus{}optimizer}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}optimizer}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{epoch}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{checkpoint\PYZus{}dir}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{training\PYZus{}checkpoints}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{    }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Save model checkpoints for later restoration.}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Args:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        generator: Generator model}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        discriminator: Discriminator model}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        g\PYZus{}optimizer: Generator optimizer}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        d\PYZus{}optimizer: Discriminator optimizer}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        epoch: Current epoch}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        checkpoint\PYZus{}dir: Directory to save checkpoint}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
    \PY{n}{checkpoint} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{\PYZob{}}
        \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{epoch}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:} \PY{n}{epoch}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{generator\PYZus{}state\PYZus{}dict}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:} \PY{n}{generator}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{state\PYZus{}dict}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{discriminator\PYZus{}state\PYZus{}dict}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:} \PY{n}{discriminator}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{state\PYZus{}dict}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{g\PYZus{}optimizer\PYZus{}state\PYZus{}dict}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:} \PY{n}{g\PYZus{}optimizer}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{state\PYZus{}dict}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}
        \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{d\PYZus{}optimizer\PYZus{}state\PYZus{}dict}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:} \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}optimizer}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{state\PYZus{}dict}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{p}{\PYZcb{}}
    \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{save}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{checkpoint}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{checkpoint\PYZus{}dir}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{/checkpoint\PYZus{}epoch\PYZus{}}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{epoch}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{.pt}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{load\PYZus{}checkpoint}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{checkpoint\PYZus{}path}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{generator}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{discriminator}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{g\PYZus{}optimizer}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}optimizer}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{    }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Load model checkpoint.}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Args:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        checkpoint\PYZus{}path: Path to checkpoint file}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        generator: Generator model}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        discriminator: Discriminator model}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        g\PYZus{}optimizer: Generator optimizer}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        d\PYZus{}optimizer: Discriminator optimizer}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Returns:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        Starting epoch number}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
    \PY{n}{checkpoint} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{load}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{checkpoint\PYZus{}path}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{map\PYZus{}location}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{generator}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{load\PYZus{}state\PYZus{}dict}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{checkpoint}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{generator\PYZus{}state\PYZus{}dict}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{discriminator}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{load\PYZus{}state\PYZus{}dict}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{checkpoint}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{discriminator\PYZus{}state\PYZus{}dict}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{g\PYZus{}optimizer}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{load\PYZus{}state\PYZus{}dict}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{checkpoint}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{g\PYZus{}optimizer\PYZus{}state\PYZus{}dict}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}optimizer}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{load\PYZus{}state\PYZus{}dict}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{checkpoint}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{d\PYZus{}optimizer\PYZus{}state\PYZus{}dict}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{checkpoint}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{epoch}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}

\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Helper Functions Defined:}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{50}\PY{p}{)}

    \begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
Helper Functions Defined:
==================================================

    \end{Verbatim}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{39}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Part 1e) Training Loop for MNIST GAN}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Task: Train the model with checkpointing, saving generated images during training}

\PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{train\PYZus{}mnist\PYZus{}gan}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{generator}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{discriminator}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{dataloader}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{num\PYZus{}epochs}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{noise\PYZus{}dim}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{,} 
                    \PY{n}{checkpoint\PYZus{}interval}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{15}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{learning\PYZus{}rate}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mf}{1e\PYZhy{}4}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{    }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Complete training loop for MNIST GAN.}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Training process:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    1. Train Discriminator:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{       \PYZhy{} Forward pass real images \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} should output approx1}
\PY{l+s+sd}{       \PYZhy{} Forward pass fake images \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} should output approx0}
\PY{l+s+sd}{       \PYZhy{} Compute loss and update discriminator}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }
\PY{l+s+sd}{    2. Train Generator:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{       \PYZhy{} Generate fake images}
\PY{l+s+sd}{       \PYZhy{} Forward pass through discriminator}
\PY{l+s+sd}{       \PYZhy{} Compute loss (want discriminator to output approx1)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{       \PYZhy{} Update generator}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Args:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        generator: Generator model}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        discriminator: Discriminator model}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        dataloader: PyTorch DataLoader with training images}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        num\PYZus{}epochs: Number of training epochs}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        noise\PYZus{}dim: Dimension of noise vector}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        device: Device to train on (cuda/cpu)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        checkpoint\PYZus{}interval: Save checkpoint every N epochs}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        learning\PYZus{}rate: Learning rate for both optimizers}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Loss function}
    \PY{n}{criterion} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{BCEWithLogitsLoss}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Optimizers (using Adam with beta1=0.5 as recommended for GANs)}
    \PY{n}{g\PYZus{}optimizer} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{optim}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Adam}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{generator}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{parameters}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{lr}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{learning\PYZus{}rate}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{betas}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.5}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mf}{0.999}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}optimizer} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{optim}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Adam}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{discriminator}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{parameters}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{lr}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{learning\PYZus{}rate}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{betas}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.5}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mf}{0.999}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Fixed noise for consistent visualization}
    \PY{n}{fixed\PYZus{}noise} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{randn}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{16}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{noise\PYZus{}dim}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Training history}
    \PY{n}{g\PYZus{}losses} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}
    \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}losses} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}
    
    \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Starting MNIST GAN Training...}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{70}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{epoch} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{range}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{num\PYZus{}epochs} \PY{o}{+} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{epoch\PYZus{}start} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{time}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{time}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{epoch\PYZus{}g\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}
        \PY{n}{epoch\PYZus{}d\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}
        \PY{n}{num\PYZus{}batches} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}
        
        \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}idx}\PY{p}{,} \PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{real\PYZus{}images}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{enumerate}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dataloader}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}size} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{real\PYZus{}images}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{size}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{real\PYZus{}images} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{real\PYZus{}images}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
            
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} ============================================}
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Train Discriminator}
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} ============================================}
            \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}optimizer}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{zero\PYZus{}grad}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
            
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Real images}
            \PY{n}{real\PYZus{}output} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{discriminator}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{real\PYZus{}images}\PY{p}{)}
            
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Fake images}
            \PY{n}{noise} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{randn}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}size}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{noise\PYZus{}dim}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}images} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{generator}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{noise}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}output} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{discriminator}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}images}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{detach}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Detach to avoid training generator}
            
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Compute discriminator loss}
            \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{discriminator\PYZus{}loss}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{real\PYZus{}output}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}output}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{criterion}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{backward}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}optimizer}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{step}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
            
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} ============================================}
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Train Generator}
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} ============================================}
            \PY{n}{g\PYZus{}optimizer}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{zero\PYZus{}grad}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
            
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Generate fake images and get discriminator\PYZsq{}s opinion}
            \PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}output} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{discriminator}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}images}\PY{p}{)}
            
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Compute generator loss}
            \PY{n}{g\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{generator\PYZus{}loss}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}output}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{criterion}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{g\PYZus{}loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{backward}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{g\PYZus{}optimizer}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{step}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
            
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Track losses}
            \PY{n}{epoch\PYZus{}g\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{+}\PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{g\PYZus{}loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{epoch\PYZus{}d\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{+}\PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{num\PYZus{}batches} \PY{o}{+}\PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}
            
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Print batch progress every 50 batches}
            \PY{k}{if} \PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}idx} \PY{o}{+} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{\PYZpc{}} \PY{l+m+mi}{50} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{:}
                \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  Batch [}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}idx}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{o}{+}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{/}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dataloader}\PY{p}{)}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{] | }\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}
                      \PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{D\PYZus{}loss: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{d\PYZus{}loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.4f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ | G\PYZus{}loss: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{g\PYZus{}loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.4f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{end}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+se}{\PYZbs{}r}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
        
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Average losses for epoch}
        \PY{n}{avg\PYZus{}g\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{epoch\PYZus{}g\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{/} \PY{n}{num\PYZus{}batches}
        \PY{n}{avg\PYZus{}d\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{epoch\PYZus{}d\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{/} \PY{n}{num\PYZus{}batches}
        \PY{n}{g\PYZus{}losses}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{append}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{avg\PYZus{}g\PYZus{}loss}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}losses}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{append}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{avg\PYZus{}d\PYZus{}loss}\PY{p}{)}
        
        \PY{n}{epoch\PYZus{}time} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{time}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{time}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{\PYZhy{}} \PY{n}{epoch\PYZus{}start}
        
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Print progress}
        \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Epoch [}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{epoch}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{/}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{num\PYZus{}epochs}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{] | }\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}
              \PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{D\PYZus{}loss: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{avg\PYZus{}d\PYZus{}loss}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.4f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ | }\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}
              \PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{G\PYZus{}loss: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{avg\PYZus{}g\PYZus{}loss}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.4f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ | }\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}
              \PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Time: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{epoch\PYZus{}time}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.2f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{s}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
        
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Generate and save images}
        \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{epoch} \PY{o}{\PYZpc{}} \PY{l+m+mi}{5} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+m+mi}{0} \PY{o+ow}{or} \PY{n}{epoch} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{n}{generate\PYZus{}and\PYZus{}save\PYZus{}images}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{generator}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{epoch}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{fixed\PYZus{}noise}\PY{p}{)}
        
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Save checkpoint}
        \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{epoch} \PY{o}{\PYZpc{}} \PY{n}{checkpoint\PYZus{}interval} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{n}{save\PYZus{}checkpoint}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{generator}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{discriminator}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{g\PYZus{}optimizer}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}optimizer}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{epoch}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  → Checkpoint saved at epoch }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{epoch}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{70}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Training Complete!}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{g\PYZus{}losses}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}losses}

\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Training Function Defined:}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{50}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{[OK] train\PYZus{}mnist\PYZus{}gan: Complete GAN training loop}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} Alternates between training discriminator and generator}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} Saves checkpoints and generates sample images}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} Tracks loss history for both networks}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}

    \subsubsection{{[}SUMMARY{]} Summary - Part 1 Classes and Functions
Complete}\label{summary-summary---part-1-classes-and-functions-complete}

\textbf{What we accomplished:} - {[}DONE{]} \textbf{MNISTGenerator}:
Transforms 100-dim noise → 28x28 grayscale images - Uses transposed
convolutions (upsampling) to increase spatial resolution - Output range:
{[}-1, 1{]} via tanh activation

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  {[}DONE{]} \textbf{MNISTDiscriminator}: Classifies 28x28 images as
  real/fake

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Uses standard convolutions (downsampling) for feature extraction
  \item
    Outputs logits (raw scores before sigmoid)
  \end{itemize}
\item
  {[}DONE{]} \textbf{Loss Functions}: Binary cross-entropy for
  adversarial training

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Discriminator: Maximize correct classification of real and fake
  \item
    Generator: Maximize discriminator's error on fake images
  \end{itemize}
\item
  {[}DONE{]} \textbf{Training Infrastructure}: Complete training loop
  with checkpointing
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Key Learning Points:} - \textbf{GAN Architecture}: Generator
upsamples, Discriminator downsamples - \textbf{Adversarial Loss}: Both
networks improve by competing against each other - \textbf{Batch
Normalization}: Stabilizes training by normalizing layer inputs -
\textbf{LeakyReLU}: Prevents dying neurons by allowing small negative
gradients - \textbf{Separate Optimizers}: Generator and Discriminator
trained independently

\textbf{Architecture Summary:}

\begin{verbatim}
Generator:     [100] → Dense → [7x7x256] → UpConv → [14x14x64] → UpConv → [28x28x1]
Discriminator: [28x28x1] → Conv → [14x14x64] → Conv → [7x7x128] → Dense → [1]
\end{verbatim}

\textbf{Next Step:} Load MNIST dataset and execute training

    \begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsection{Section 3: Part 1 - MNIST Data Loading and Training
Execution}\label{section-3-part-1---mnist-data-loading-and-training-execution}

\subsubsection{Purpose:}\label{purpose}

Load the MNIST dataset, initialize models, and execute the GAN training
process.

\subsubsection{Dataset Information:}\label{dataset-information}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{MNIST}: 60,000 training images of handwritten digits (0-9)
\item
  \textbf{Image Size}: 28x28 grayscale
\item
  \textbf{Normalization}: Scale from {[}0, 255{]} to {[}-1, 1{]} to
  match generator's tanh output
\end{itemize}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{40}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Part 1a) Load and prepare MNIST dataset}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Task: Import MNIST dataset from torchvision (Keras equivalent), reshape and normalize images}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Define transformations: Convert to tensor and normalize to [\PYZhy{}1, 1]}
\PY{n}{transform} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{transforms}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Compose}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}
    \PY{n}{transforms}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{ToTensor}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Convert PIL image to tensor [0, 1]}
    \PY{n}{transforms}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Normalize}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.5}\PY{p}{,}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,} \PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.5}\PY{p}{,}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Normalize to [\PYZhy{}1, 1]: (x \PYZhy{} 0.5) / 0.5}
\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Load MNIST dataset}
\PY{n}{train\PYZus{}dataset} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torchvision}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{datasets}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{MNIST}\PY{p}{(}
    \PY{n}{root}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{./dataset}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{n}{train}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{n}{download}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{n}{transform}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{transform}
\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Create DataLoader}
\PY{n}{BATCH\PYZus{}SIZE} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{256}
\PY{n}{BUFFER\PYZus{}SIZE} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{60000}

\PY{n}{train\PYZus{}loader} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{DataLoader}\PY{p}{(}
    \PY{n}{train\PYZus{}dataset}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}size}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{BATCH\PYZus{}SIZE}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{n}{shuffle}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{n}{num\PYZus{}workers}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{,}
    \PY{n}{pin\PYZus{}memory}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True} \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{cuda}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{is\PYZus{}available}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)} \PY{k}{else} \PY{k+kc}{False}
\PY{p}{)}

\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Probability (real): }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{probability}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.4f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{The discriminator thinks this image is }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{probability}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{*}\PY{l+m+mi}{100}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.1f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZpc{} real.}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Since the generator is untrained, this is essentially a random guess.}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{The discriminator will learn to detect fake images during training.}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}

    \subsubsection{{[}SUMMARY{]} Summary - Testing Untrained Generator and
Discriminator}\label{summary-summary---testing-untrained-generator-and-discriminator}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Untrained Generator Output}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Input: Random noise vector (100 numbers from N(0,1))
  \item
    Output: Random-looking 28x28 image
  \item
    \textbf{It looks like noise}: The generator hasn't learned any
    patterns yet! Its weights are randomly initialized, so it's
    essentially applying random transformations to random input.
  \item
    Think of it like asking someone who's never seen writing to draw a
    digit - they'd produce scribbles.
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Discriminator's Initial Response}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    The discriminator outputs a probability (approx50\% in most cases
    initially)
  \item
    \textbf{Why approx50\%?} With random weights, the discriminator
    can't distinguish real from fake yet - it's guessing randomly like a
    coin flip.
  \item
    The logit (raw output before sigmoid) is near 0, which becomes
    approx0.5 after sigmoid transformation.
  \end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{The Adversarial Game Begins:}

Before training, we have: - \textbf{Generator}: Creates meaningless
noise patterns - \textbf{Discriminator}: Can't tell real from fake
(random guessing)

During training: - \textbf{Phase 1 (Early epochs)}: Discriminator
quickly learns to identify obvious fakes - Generator improves slightly,
discriminator gets very confident - Discriminator loss decreases,
Generator loss increases

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Phase 2 (Middle epochs)}: Generator starts creating digit-like
  patterns

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Discriminator has to work harder to distinguish real from fake
  \item
    Both networks improve, losses oscillate
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Phase 3 (Late epochs)}: Nash equilibrium approached

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Generator creates convincing fakes
  \item
    Discriminator can barely distinguish them (accuracy → 50\%)
  \item
    Both losses stabilize
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Key Insight - The Adversarial Process:}

This is like an art forger (Generator) vs an art expert (Discriminator):
1. \textbf{Initial stage}: Forger creates terrible fake, expert easily
spots it 2. \textbf{After and short while}: Forger improves, expert
learns to spot new tricks 3. \textbf{Some time later}: Forger gets good,
expert must look carefully 4. \textbf{Near the end of training}:
Forger's work nearly indistinguishable, expert can only guess

The competition drives both to excellence under the right conditions!

\textbf{Mathematical Formulation:}

The GAN trains by solving this minimax game:

\begin{verbatim}
min_G max_D V(D,G) = E[log(D(x))] + E[log(1 - D(G(z)))]
\end{verbatim}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Discriminator} maximizes: log(D(real)) + log(1-D(fake))

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Wants D(real) -\textgreater{} 1 and D(fake) -\textgreater{} 0
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Generator} minimizes: log(1 - D(G(z)))

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Equivalent to maximizing log(D(G(z)))
  \item
    Wants D(fake) -\textgreater{} 1 (fool the discriminator)
  \end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{43}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Part 1e) Train the MNIST GAN}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Task: Execute training with checkpointing, save model, create GIF of training progression}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Training parameters}
\PY{n}{NUM\PYZus{}EPOCHS} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{50}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Use 50 epochs for better results (can reduce to 20 for faster testing)}

\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} Wild oscillations indicate training instability}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} If D loss → 0 and G loss → ∞, discriminator is too strong}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} If D loss → ∞ and G loss → 0, generator collapsed (mode collapse)}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    \begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
Loss Analysis:
==================================================
Final Generator Loss: 0.8424
Final Discriminator Loss: 1.2921

[NOTE] What to look for:
  - Both losses should stabilize (not necessarily converge)
  - Discriminator loss approx1.4 and Generator loss approx0.7-2.0 is typical
  - Wild oscillations indicate training instability
  - If D loss → 0 and G loss → ∞, discriminator is too strong
  - If D loss → ∞ and G loss → 0, generator collapsed (mode collapse)
    \end{Verbatim}

    \subsubsection{{[}SUMMARY{]} Summary - MNIST GAN Training
Results}\label{summary-summary---mnist-gan-training-results}

\textbf{Training Outcome:} After 50 epochs, our GAN has learned to
generate digit-like images, but \textbf{the results are not perfect} -
and that's completely normal for GANs!

\textbf{What We Achieved:} - {[}DONE{]} Generator learned to produce
structured patterns instead of random noise - {[}DONE{]} Generated
images resemble handwritten digits - {[}DONE{]} Training losses
stabilized (both networks reached equilibrium) - {[}DONE{]} No
catastrophic mode collapse occurred

\textbf{Why Results Aren't Perfect:}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Architecture Limitations}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Relatively simple network (fewer parameters than state-of-the-art
    models)
  \item
    Only 50 epochs of training (modern GANs often train for 1000+
    epochs)
  \item
    Basic loss function (more advanced techniques exist: Wasserstein,
    StyleGAN, etc.)
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Common GAN Artifacts You'll Notice}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{Blurriness}: Some digits lack sharp edges (GANs tend to
    average features)
  \item
    \textbf{Irregular shapes}: Occasional malformed digits or ambiguous
    numbers
  \item
    \textbf{Inconsistent quality}: Some outputs great, others poor
    (normal GAN behavior)
  \item
    \textbf{Limited diversity}: May favor certain digit styles over
    others
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Training Instability Signs}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Loss curves oscillate rather than smoothly decreasing
  \item
    Generator and Discriminator constantly competing (never truly
    converge)
  \item
    Some epochs produce better outputs than others
  \end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Real-World Expectations:} - {[}GOAL{]} \textbf{Goal}: Generate
recognizable digits ({[}OK{]} Achieved!) - {[}no-entry{]} \textbf{Not
Expected}: Perfect, photorealistic digits at this complexity level -
{[}chart{]} \textbf{Improvement Paths}: - Longer training (100-200
epochs) - Deeper networks (more layers/parameters) - Advanced techniques
(Spectral Normalization, Progressive Growing) - Better loss functions
(WGAN-GP, Hinge Loss)

\textbf{The Key Takeaway:} GANs are notoriously difficult to train.
Getting ``reasonable'' results (like we did) is actually a success!
Perfect generation requires: - Much larger models - Significantly more
training time - Advanced stabilization techniques - Careful
hyperparameter tuning

The fact that our simple GAN learned anything meaningful demonstrates
the power of adversarial training.

    \subsubsection{{[}SUMMARY{]} Summary - Part 1 Training
Complete}\label{summary-summary---part-1-training-complete}

\textbf{What we accomplished:} - {[}DONE{]} Loaded MNIST dataset with
60,000 training images - {[}DONE{]} Normalized images to {[}-1, 1{]}
range (matching generator output) - {[}DONE{]} Created DataLoader with
batching and shuffling - {[}DONE{]} Initialized Generator and
Discriminator models - {[}DONE{]} Tested untrained generator (produced
random noise) - {[}DONE{]} Executed complete GAN training loop for 50
epochs - {[}DONE{]} Visualized loss curves showing training dynamics

\textbf{Key Learning Points:}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Data Preparation}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    MNIST normalization: (pixel/255 - 0.5) / 0.5 = {[}-1, 1{]}
  \item
    Matches tanh activation range in generator
  \item
    DataLoader handles batching and shuffling automatically
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Training Dynamics}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{Before training}: Generator produces random noise
  \item
    \textbf{During training}: Both networks improve through competition
  \item
    \textbf{After training}: Generator creates realistic-looking digits
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Loss Interpretation}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{Discriminator Loss}: How well it distinguishes real vs fake
  \item
    \textbf{Generator Loss}: How well it fools the discriminator
  \item
    \textbf{Healthy training}: Both losses stabilize (oscillation is
    normal)
  \item
    \textbf{Unhealthy training}: One loss dominates (imbalanced power)
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{GAN Challenges}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{Mode Collapse}: Generator produces limited variety
  \item
    \textbf{Training Instability}: Losses oscillate wildly
  \item
    \textbf{Hyperparameter Sensitivity}: Learning rate, architecture
    balance critical
  \end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

    \begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

        \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{f} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{all\PYZus{}files}\PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{:}\PY{l+m+mi}{10}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{    \PYZhy{} }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{k}{else}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+se}{\PYZbs{}n}\PY{l+s+s2}{  Directory }\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{generated\PYZus{}images/}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ does not exist!}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}

    \subsubsection{{[}THINKING{]} Understanding the GIF
Evolution}\label{thinking-understanding-the-gif-evolution}

\textbf{What you should observe in the animation:}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Early Epochs (1-10)}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Images start as random noise patterns
  \item
    Some vague blob-like shapes begin to emerge
  \item
    No clear digit structure yet
  \item
    Generator is learning basic contrast and spatial patterns
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Middle Epochs (11-30)}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Digit-like shapes become recognizable
  \item
    Edges sharpen and structure improves
  \item
    Some digits look better than others
  \item
    Generator refining its understanding of digit features
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Late Epochs (31-50)}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Clear, well-formed digits appear
  \item
    Consistent style and quality
  \item
    Fine details emerge (curves, loops, strokes)
  \item
    Generator has learned the MNIST digit manifold
  \end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Key Insights:} - The GIF demonstrates \textbf{progressive
learning} - the generator gradually improves - \textbf{No explicit
supervision} on what digits look like - it learns from the
discriminator's feedback - The \textbf{adversarial process} drives both
networks to improve - Some variability in quality is normal (not all
generated digits will be perfect)

\textbf{This visualization is powerful because} it shows the generator
``discovering'' what handwritten digits look like purely through
competition with the discriminator!

    \subsubsection{{[}SUMMARY{]} Summary - Part 1
Complete}\label{summary-summary---part-1-complete}

\textbf{What we accomplished in Part 1:} - {[}DONE{]} Built a complete
GAN from scratch in PyTorch - {[}DONE{]} Trained on MNIST dataset
(60,000 images) - {[}DONE{]} Generated realistic handwritten digits from
random noise - {[}DONE{]} Visualized training evolution through GIF
animation - {[}DONE{]} Analyzed loss curves and training dynamics

\textbf{Major Learning Achievements:}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{GAN Fundamentals}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Generator: Noise → Image transformation using transposed
    convolutions
  \item
    Discriminator: Image → Real/Fake classification using convolutions
  \item
    Adversarial Training: Both networks improve through competition
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{PyTorch Implementation}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Custom nn.Module classes for flexible architecture
  \item
    Separate optimizers for generator and discriminator
  \item
    Proper gradient handling (detach for discriminator training)
  \item
    Checkpoint saving and model persistence
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Training Insights}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    GANs require careful balance between G and D
  \item
    Loss values don't directly indicate quality (visual inspection
    needed)
  \item
    Progressive improvement visible through generated samples
  \item
    Training can be unstable (mode collapse, oscillations)
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Practical Skills}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Data normalization to match activation functions
  \item
    Batch processing with DataLoader
  \item
    GPU acceleration for faster training
  \item
    Visualization and progress monitoring
  \end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Key Takeaway:} GANs learn implicit representations of data
distributions without explicit pixel-level supervision. The generator
never sees real images directly - it only receives gradients from the
discriminator!

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\textbf{Next: Part 2} - We'll build a more advanced DCGAN (Deep
Convolutional GAN) to generate Pokemon characters with colored 64x64
images.

    \begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsection{Section 5: Part 2 - Pokemon DCGAN
Architecture}\label{section-5-part-2---pokemon-dcgan-architecture}

\subsubsection{Purpose:}\label{purpose}

Implement a Deep Convolutional GAN (DCGAN) to generate Pokemon character
images.

\subsubsection{Key Differences from Part
1:}\label{key-differences-from-part-1}

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Image Size}: 64x64 (vs 28x28 for MNIST)
\item
  \textbf{Color}: RGB 3-channel (vs grayscale 1-channel)
\item
  \textbf{Architecture}: Deeper network with modular blocks
\item
  \textbf{Dataset}: Pokemon sprites (more complex than digits)
\end{itemize}

\subsubsection{DCGAN Architecture
Principles:}\label{dcgan-architecture-principles}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Use strided convolutions} instead of pooling
\item
  \textbf{Batch normalization} in both generator and discriminator
\item
  \textbf{ReLU activation} in generator (except output layer uses tanh)
\item
  \textbf{LeakyReLU activation} in discriminator
\item
  \textbf{No fully connected layers} (except for generator input
  projection)
\end{enumerate}

\PY{k}{class}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nc}{G\PYZus{}block}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Module}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{    }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Generator building block: TransposedConv2d + BatchNorm + ReLU}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }
\PY{l+s+sd}{    This block upsamples the spatial dimensions (increases height and width)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    while adjusting the number of channels.}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }

\PY{l+s+sd}{        in\PYZus{}channels: Number of input channels}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        out\PYZus{}channels: Number of output channels}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        kernel\PYZus{}size: Size of convolutional kernel}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        stride: Stride for transposed convolution (controls upsampling rate)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        padding: Padding for convolution}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf+fm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}init\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{in\PYZus{}channels}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{out\PYZus{}channels}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{kernel\PYZus{}size}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{4}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{stride}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{padding}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n+nb}{super}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{G\PYZus{}block}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n+nf+fm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}init\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{conv\PYZus{}transpose} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{ConvTranspose2d}\PY{p}{(}
            \PY{n}{in\PYZus{}channels}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{out\PYZus{}channels}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{kernel\PYZus{}size}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{stride}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{padding}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{bias}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{False}
        \PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}norm} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{BatchNorm2d}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{out\PYZus{}channels}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{activation} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{ReLU}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{inplace}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{forward}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{conv\PYZus{}transpose}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}norm}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{activation}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{x}

\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} BatchNorm: Normalizes activations for stable training}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
