\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} No bias in conv: BatchNorm makes bias redundant}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} No bias in conv: BatchNorm makes bias redundant}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    \subsubsection{{[}SUMMARY{]} Summary - G\_block (Generator Building
Block)}\label{summary-summary---g_block-generator-building-block}

\textbf{Purpose}: G\_block is a reusable module that upsamples feature
maps in the generator.

\textbf{Key Components}: 1. \textbf{TransposedConv2d}: Learnable
upsampling (stride=2 doubles spatial dimensions) 2.
\textbf{BatchNorm2d}: Normalizes activations for stable training 3.
\textbf{ReLU}: Non-linear activation for complex transformations

\textbf{Example}: Input (3, 16, 16) → Output (20, 32, 32) - Spatial:
16x16 → 32x32 (2x upsampling) - Channels: 3 → 20 (transforms features)

This block will be stacked multiple times in the generator to
progressively grow from small noise to full-size Pokemon images.

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{ }{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Part 2e) Discriminator Block (D\PYZus{}block) for DCGAN}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Task: Create discriminator block for classifying images}

\PY{k}{class}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nc}{D\PYZus{}block}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Module}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{    }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Discriminator building block: Conv2d + BatchNorm + LeakyReLU}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }
\PY{l+s+sd}{    This block downsamples the spatial dimensions (decreases height and width)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    while increasing the number of channels for feature extraction.}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Args:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        in\PYZus{}channels: Number of input channels}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        out\PYZus{}channels: Number of output channels}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        kernel\PYZus{}size: Size of convolutional kernel}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        stride: Stride for convolution (controls downsampling rate)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        padding: Padding for convolution}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        alpha: Negative slope for LeakyReLU}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf+fm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}init\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{in\PYZus{}channels}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{out\PYZus{}channels}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{kernel\PYZus{}size}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{4}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{stride}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{padding}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{alpha}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.2}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n+nb}{super}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{D\PYZus{}block}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n+nf+fm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}init\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{conv} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Conv2d}\PY{p}{(}
            \PY{n}{in\PYZus{}channels}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{out\PYZus{}channels}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{kernel\PYZus{}size}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{stride}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{padding}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{bias}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{False}
        \PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}norm} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{BatchNorm2d}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{out\PYZus{}channels}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{activation} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{LeakyReLU}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{alpha}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{inplace}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{forward}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{conv}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}norm}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{activation}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{x}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Part 2e continued) Test D\PYZus{}block with zeros input}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Task: Import zeros signal (stride=2, 16x16x3), call D\PYZus{}block with 20 layers, check output shape}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Testing D\PYZus{}block:}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{50}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}input} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{zeros}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{3}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{16}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{16}\PY{p}{)}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} x = tf.zeros((2, 16, 16, 3)) equivalent}
\PY{n}{d\PYZus{}block\PYZus{}test} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{D\PYZus{}block}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{in\PYZus{}channels}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{3}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{out\PYZus{}channels}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{20}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}output} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}block\PYZus{}test}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}input}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Input shape:  }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}input}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{shape}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Output shape: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}output}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{shape}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Downsampling: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}input}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{shape}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{]}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{x}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}input}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{shape}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{3}\PY{p}{]}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ → }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}output}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{shape}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{]}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{x}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}output}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{shape}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{3}\PY{p}{]}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Channels: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}input}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{shape}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{]}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ → }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{test\PYZus{}output}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{shape}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{]}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+se}{\PYZbs{}n}\PY{l+s+s2}{[NOTE] Understanding D\PYZus{}block:}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} Conv2d: Downsamples spatial dimensions (stride=2 halves size)}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} BatchNorm: Normalizes for stable discriminator training}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} LeakyReLU: Allows small negative gradients (\PYZdl{}}\PY{l+s+se}{\PYZbs{}a}\PY{l+s+s2}{lpha\PYZdl{}=0.2)}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{    • Prevents }\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{dying ReLU}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ problem}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{    • Helps discriminator learn from negative features}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{    • Helps discriminator learn from negative features}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

        \PY{n+nb}{super}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{PokemonGenerator}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n+nf+fm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}init\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{p}{)}

        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{block2} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{G\PYZus{}block}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{ngf} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{4}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{ngf} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{)}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} (8,8,256) \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} (16,16,128)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{block3} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{G\PYZus{}block}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{ngf} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{ngf}\PY{p}{)}      \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} (16,16,128) \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} (32,32,64)}

        \PY{p}{)}

        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{initial}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{z}\PY{p}{)}   \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} (batch, 512, 4, 4)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{block1}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} (batch, 256, 8, 8)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{block2}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} (batch, 128, 16, 16)}

    \subsubsection{{[}SUMMARY{]} Summary - Pokemon Generator
Architecture}\label{summary-summary---pokemon-generator-architecture}

\textbf{Generator Purpose:} The Pokemon Generator creates 64x64 RGB
images (colored Pokemon sprites) from 100-dimensional random noise. This
is significantly more complex than MNIST generation due to: -
\textbf{Higher resolution}: 64x64 vs 28x28 (4x more pixels per
dimension) - \textbf{Color images}: 3 channels (RGB) vs 1 channel
(grayscale) - \textbf{Complex textures}: Pokemon have varied colors,
patterns, eyes, limbs, etc.

\textbf{Architecture Breakdown:}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Input}: (batch, 100, 1, 1) - 100D noise vector reshaped to 4D
  tensor

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Same latent dimension as MNIST for consistency
  \item
    Extra dimensions (1x1) allow convolutional operations
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Initial Projection}: (100, 1, 1) → (512, 4, 4)

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Transposed conv with stride=1 (no upsampling yet)
  \item
    Creates initial 4x4 spatial map with 512 feature channels
  \item
    Think of it as a ``sketch'' with lots of information (512 channels)
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{G\_block 1}: (512, 4, 4) → (256, 8, 8) {[}2x UPSAMPLING{]}

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Doubles spatial size: 4→8
  \item
    Halves channels: 512→256
  \item
    Begins building spatial structure
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{G\_block 2}: (256, 8, 8) → (128, 16, 16) {[}2x UPSAMPLING{]}

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Continues upsampling: 8→16
  \item
    Further reduces channels: 256→128
  \item
    Adding finer details
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{G\_block 3}: (128, 16, 16) → (64, 32, 32) {[}2x UPSAMPLING{]}

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Third upsampling: 16→32
  \item
    Channels: 128→64
  \item
    Refining features (eyes, limbs, patterns)
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Final Layer}: (64, 32, 32) → (3, 64, 64) {[}2x UPSAMPLING{]}

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Last upsampling: 32→64 (target size)
  \item
    Reduces to 3 channels (RGB)
  \item
    Tanh activation: outputs range {[}-1, 1{]}
  \end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Key DCGAN Design Principles:}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Progressive Upsampling}: 1x1 → 4x4 → 8x8 → 16x16 → 32x32 →
  64x64

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Gradual refinement from coarse to fine details
  \item
    Each step doubles spatial resolution
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Channel Reduction}: 512 → 256 → 128 → 64 → 3

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Start with many feature channels (high-level concepts)
  \item
    Gradually reduce to final RGB (low-level pixels)
  \item
    Opposite of discriminator (which expands channels)
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Fractionally-Strided Convolutions}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Transposed convolutions with stride=2 for upsampling
  \item
    Learnable upsampling (better than interpolation)
  \item
    No pooling/unpooling layers
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Batch Normalization Everywhere} (except output):

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Stabilizes training of deep networks
  \item
    Prevents covariate shift
  \item
    Critical for DCGAN convergence
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{ReLU Activation}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Used in generator (not LeakyReLU)
  \item
    Encourages sparse representations
  \item
    Helps create sharp features
  \end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Parameter Count}: approx1.5-2 million (much larger than MNIST
Generator) - More parameters needed for complex color images - Deeper
network (more layers) - Higher resolution output

\textbf{Training Challenge:} Generating convincing Pokemon is harder
than MNIST digits because: - More visual diversity (700+ Pokemon vs 10
digits) - Complex color patterns and textures - Recognizable features
required (eyes, limbs, body structure) - Higher resolution means more
room for artifacts

    \subsubsection{{[}SUMMARY{]} Summary - Pokemon Discriminator
Architecture}\label{summary-summary---pokemon-discriminator-architecture}

\textbf{Discriminator Purpose:} The Pokemon Discriminator is a binary
classifier that distinguishes between: - \textbf{Real Pokemon}:
Authentic sprites from the dataset - \textbf{Fake Pokemon}: Generated
images from the Generator

This is the ``critic'' that provides feedback to train the generator.

\textbf{Architecture Breakdown:}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Input}: (batch, 3, 64, 64) - 64x64 RGB image

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Can be real Pokemon sprite or generator output
  \item
    3 channels for full color information
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Initial Layer}: (3, 64, 64) → (64, 32, 32)

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    First convolution: stride=2 halves spatial dimensions
  \item
    3→64 channels (begin feature extraction)
  \item
    \textbf{No BatchNorm} on first layer (DCGAN guideline)
  \item
    LeakyReLU activation (\(\alpha\)=0.2)
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{D\_block 1}: (64, 32, 32) → (128, 16, 16) {[}2x
  DOWNSAMPLING{]}

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Halves spatial size: 32→16
  \item
    Doubles channels: 64→128
  \item
    Extracts mid-level features (colors, edges)
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{D\_block 2}: (128, 16, 16) → (256, 8, 8) {[}2x DOWNSAMPLING{]}

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Continues downsampling: 16→8
  \item
    Doubles channels: 128→256
  \item
    Captures high-level features (shapes, patterns)
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{D\_block 3}: (256, 8, 8) → (512, 4, 4) {[}2x DOWNSAMPLING{]}

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Final downsampling: 8→4
  \item
    Doubles channels: 256→512
  \item
    Very abstract features (Pokemon-ness, realism)
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Final Classification}: (512, 4, 4) → (1, 1, 1)

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Convolution with kernel\_size=4 eliminates spatial dimensions
  \item
    512 feature maps → single logit
  \item
    Output: real/fake score (no sigmoid, use BCEWithLogitsLoss)
  \end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Key DCGAN Design Principles:}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Progressive Downsampling}: 64x64 → 32x32 → 16x16 → 8x8 → 4x4 →
  1x1

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Mirror of generator's upsampling
  \item
    Hierarchical feature extraction
  \item
    Each step halves spatial resolution
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Channel Expansion}: 3 → 64 → 128 → 256 → 512 → 1

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Start with RGB pixels (low-level)
  \item
    Expand to many feature channels (high-level concepts)
  \item
    Opposite of generator (which reduces channels)
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Strided Convolutions} (No Pooling):

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Stride=2 for downsampling instead of max pooling
  \item
    Lets network learn optimal downsampling
  \item
    More parameters = better feature learning
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{LeakyReLU Throughout}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \(\alpha\)=0.2 (allows 20\% of negative gradients)
  \item
    Prevents dying neurons
  \item
    Better for discriminators than regular ReLU
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{No BatchNorm on Input Layer}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    DCGAN guideline for stability
  \item
    First layer processes raw pixels directly
  \item
    BatchNorm in all subsequent layers
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{No Sigmoid Output}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Returns raw logits (unnormalized scores)
  \item
    BCEWithLogitsLoss applies sigmoid internally
  \item
    More numerically stable
  \end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Parameter Count}: approx2.7 million (similar to Generator) -
Slightly more parameters than Generator - Symmetric architecture (mirror
image) - Both networks need comparable capacity

\textbf{Training Role:}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Early Training}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Easily spots fake Pokemon (generator is weak)
  \item
    Provides strong gradients for generator improvement
  \item
    Loss decreases quickly
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Mid Training}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Generator improves, discriminator must work harder
  \item
    Learns subtle differences (texture quality, color consistency)
  \item
    Loss stabilizes around 0.5-1.0
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Late Training}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Both networks are equally matched
  \item
    Discriminator accuracy → 50\% (can barely tell real from fake)
  \item
    This equilibrium indicates successful training
  \end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Why This Architecture Works:} - \textbf{Hierarchical Learning}:
Low-level (colors) → Mid-level (shapes) → High-level (Pokemon features)
- \textbf{Sufficient Capacity}: 2.7M parameters can learn complex
patterns - \textbf{Stable Training}: BatchNorm + LeakyReLU prevent
training issues - \textbf{Matched Power}: Similar size to Generator
(balanced adversarial game)

\textbf{Comparison to MNIST Discriminator:} - \textbf{Much Deeper}: 5
layers vs 3 layers (more complex task) - \textbf{More Channels}: Up to
512 vs 128 (richer features) - \textbf{More Parameters}: 2.7M vs 400K
(8x larger) - \textbf{Color Input}: RGB vs grayscale (3x more input
dimensions)

\PY{l+s+sd}{    → D\PYZus{}block: (32,32,64) \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} (16,16,128)}

    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf+fm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}init\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{ndf}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{64}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n+nb}{super}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{PokemonDiscriminator}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n+nf+fm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}init\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} ndf = number of discriminator filters in first conv layer}
        
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Initial layer (no batch norm on first layer as per DCGAN guidelines)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{initial} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Sequential}\PY{p}{(}
            \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Conv2d}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{3}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{ndf}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{kernel\PYZus{}size}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{4}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{stride}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{padding}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{bias}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{False}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}
            \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{LeakyReLU}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{inplace}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{p}{)}
        
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Downsampling blocks}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{block1} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{D\PYZus{}block}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{ndf}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{ndf} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{)}      \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} (32,32,64) \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} (16,16,128)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{block2} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{D\PYZus{}block}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{ndf} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{ndf} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{4}\PY{p}{)}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} (16,16,128) \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} (8,8,256)}
        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{block3} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{D\PYZus{}block}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{ndf} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{4}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{ndf} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{8}\PY{p}{)}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} (8,8,256) \PYZhy{}\PYZgt{} (4,4,512)}
        
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Final classification layer}

    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{forward}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{        }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        Args:}

        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{initial}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}   \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} (batch, 64, 32, 32)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{block1}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} (batch, 128, 16, 16)}

        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{block3}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} (batch, 512, 4, 4)}
        \PY{n}{x} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{final}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{)}     \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} (batch, 1, 1, 1)}
        \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{x}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{view}\PY{p}{(}\PY{o}{\PYZhy{}}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Flatten to (batch, 1)}

\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} e) Weight Initialization for DCGAN}

\PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{weights\PYZus{}init\PYZus{}dcgan}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{m}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{    }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Initialize network weights according to DCGAN paper recommendations.}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZhy{} Conv layers: Normal distribution (mean=0, std=0.02)}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZhy{} BatchNorm layers: Normal distribution for weights (mean=1, std=0.02), zeros for bias}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }
\PY{l+s+sd}{    This initialization is crucial for stable DCGAN training.}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
    \PY{n}{classname} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{m}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n+nv+vm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}class\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n+nv+vm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}name\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}
    \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{classname}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{find}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Conv}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{!=} \PY{o}{\PYZhy{}}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{init}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{normal\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{m}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{weight}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{data}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mf}{0.0}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mf}{0.02}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{k}{elif} \PY{n}{classname}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{find}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{BatchNorm}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{!=} \PY{o}{\PYZhy{}}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{init}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{normal\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{m}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{weight}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{data}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mf}{1.0}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mf}{0.02}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{init}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{constant\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{m}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{bias}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{data}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Weight Initialization Function Defined:}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{50}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{[OK] weights\PYZus{}init\PYZus{}dcgan: Initializes Conv and BatchNorm layers}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} Conv weights: Normal(0, 0.02)}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} BatchNorm weights: Normal(1, 0.02)}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} BatchNorm bias: 0}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+se}{\PYZbs{}n}\PY{l+s+s2}{[NOTE] Why this matters:}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} Proper initialization prevents vanishing/exploding gradients}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} Standard deviations from DCGAN paper (empirically proven)}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} Critical for GAN convergence}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Part 2f) Training Function for Pokemon DCGAN}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Task: The training function is provided \PYZhy{} explain your understanding of the training block}

\PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf}{train\PYZus{}pokemon\PYZus{}dcgan}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{netG}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{netD}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{dataloader}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{num\PYZus{}epochs}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{latent\PYZus{}dim}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{lr}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.0005}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
\PY{+w}{    }\PY{l+s+sd}{\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Training loop for Pokemon DCGAN with real\PYZhy{}time visualization.}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Args:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        netG: Generator network}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        netD: Discriminator network}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        dataloader: Pokemon image dataloader}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        num\PYZus{}epochs: Number of training epochs}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        latent\PYZus{}dim: Dimension of noise vector}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        device: Training device}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        lr: Learning rate}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    }
\PY{l+s+sd}{    Returns:}
\PY{l+s+sd}{        Lists of generator and discriminator losses}
\PY{l+s+sd}{    \PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}\PYZdq{}}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Loss function}
    \PY{n}{criterion} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{nn}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{BCEWithLogitsLoss}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Optimizers (beta1=0.5 is standard for DCGANs)}
    \PY{n}{optimizerD} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{optim}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Adam}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{netD}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{parameters}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{lr}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{lr}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{betas}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.5}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mf}{0.999}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{optimizerG} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{optim}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Adam}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{netG}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{parameters}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{lr}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{lr}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{betas}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mf}{0.5}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mf}{0.999}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Fixed noise for visualization}
    \PY{n}{fixed\PYZus{}noise} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{randn}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{21}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{latent\PYZus{}dim}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Tracking}
    \PY{n}{G\PYZus{}losses} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}
    \PY{n}{D\PYZus{}losses} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}
    
    \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Starting Pokemon DCGAN Training...}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{70}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{epoch} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{range}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{num\PYZus{}epochs} \PY{o}{+} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{epoch\PYZus{}start} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{time}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{time}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{epoch\PYZus{}G\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}
        \PY{n}{epoch\PYZus{}D\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}
        \PY{n}{num\PYZus{}batches} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}
        
        \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{i}\PY{p}{,} \PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{real\PYZus{}images}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{enumerate}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dataloader}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}size} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{real\PYZus{}images}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{size}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{real\PYZus{}images} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{real\PYZus{}images}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
            
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} ============================================}
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Train Discriminator}
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} ============================================}
            \PY{n}{netD}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{zero\PYZus{}grad}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
            
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Real images}
            \PY{n}{real\PYZus{}labels} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{ones}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}size}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{real\PYZus{}output} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{netD}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{real\PYZus{}images}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}loss\PYZus{}real} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{criterion}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{real\PYZus{}output}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{real\PYZus{}labels}\PY{p}{)}
            
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Fake images}
            \PY{n}{noise} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{randn}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}size}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{latent\PYZus{}dim}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}images} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{netG}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{noise}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}labels} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{zeros}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}size}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{device}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}output} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{netD}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}images}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{detach}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}loss\PYZus{}fake} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{criterion}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}output}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}labels}\PY{p}{)}
            
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Total discriminator loss}
            \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}loss\PYZus{}real} \PY{o}{+} \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}loss\PYZus{}fake}
            \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{backward}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{optimizerD}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{step}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
            
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} ============================================}
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Train Generator}
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} ============================================}
            \PY{n}{netG}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{zero\PYZus{}grad}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
            
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Generator wants discriminator to think fakes are real}
            \PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}output} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{netD}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}images}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{g\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{criterion}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}output}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{real\PYZus{}labels}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{g\PYZus{}loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{backward}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{optimizerG}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{step}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
            
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Track losses}
            \PY{n}{epoch\PYZus{}G\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{+}\PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{g\PYZus{}loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{epoch\PYZus{}D\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{+}\PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{d\PYZus{}loss}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{item}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{num\PYZus{}batches} \PY{o}{+}\PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}
        
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Average losses}
        \PY{n}{avg\PYZus{}G\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{epoch\PYZus{}G\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{/} \PY{n}{num\PYZus{}batches}
        \PY{n}{avg\PYZus{}D\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{epoch\PYZus{}D\PYZus{}loss} \PY{o}{/} \PY{n}{num\PYZus{}batches}
        \PY{n}{G\PYZus{}losses}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{append}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{avg\PYZus{}G\PYZus{}loss}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{D\PYZus{}losses}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{append}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{avg\PYZus{}D\PYZus{}loss}\PY{p}{)}
        
        \PY{n}{epoch\PYZus{}time} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{time}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{time}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{\PYZhy{}} \PY{n}{epoch\PYZus{}start}
        
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Print progress}
        \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Epoch [}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{epoch}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{/}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{num\PYZus{}epochs}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{] | }\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}
              \PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{D\PYZus{}loss: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{avg\PYZus{}D\PYZus{}loss}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.4f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ | }\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}
              \PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{G\PYZus{}loss: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{avg\PYZus{}G\PYZus{}loss}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.4f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ | }\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}
              \PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Time: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{epoch\PYZus{}time}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s2}{.2f}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{s}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
        
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Generate samples for visualization}
        \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{epoch} \PY{o}{\PYZpc{}} \PY{l+m+mi}{5} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+m+mi}{0} \PY{o+ow}{or} \PY{n}{epoch} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{n}{netG}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{eval}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{k}{with} \PY{n}{torch}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{no\PYZus{}grad}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
                \PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}samples} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{netG}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{fixed\PYZus{}noise}\PY{p}{)}
                \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Denormalize for display}
                \PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}samples} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}samples} \PY{o}{+} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{/} \PY{l+m+mi}{2}
                
                \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Create grid display}
                \PY{n}{grid} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{make\PYZus{}grid}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{fake\PYZus{}samples}\PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{:}\PY{l+m+mi}{16}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{nrow}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{4}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{padding}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{normalize}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{False}\PY{p}{)}
                
                \PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{figure}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{figsize}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{8}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{8}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
                \PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{imshow}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{grid}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{permute}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{cpu}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{numpy}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
                \PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{title}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Generated Pokemon \PYZhy{} Epoch }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{epoch}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
                \PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{axis}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{off}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
                \PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{tight\PYZus{}layout}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
                \PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{savefig}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{pokemon\PYZus{}generated/epoch\PYZus{}}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{epoch}\PY{l+s+si}{:}\PY{l+s+s1}{04d}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{.png}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
                \PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{show}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{netG}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{train}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{70}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Pokemon DCGAN Training Complete!}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{G\PYZus{}losses}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{D\PYZus{}losses}

\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Pokemon DCGAN Training Function Defined:}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}} \PY{o}{*} \PY{l+m+mi}{50}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{[OK] train\PYZus{}pokemon\PYZus{}dcgan: Complete training loop with visualization}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} Generates sample images every 5 epochs}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} Tracks and returns loss history}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{  \PYZhy{} Uses DCGAN\PYZhy{}specific hyperparameters (beta1=0.5)}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    \subsubsection{{[}SUMMARY{]} Summary - Part 2 Classes and Functions
Complete}\label{summary-summary---part-2-classes-and-functions-complete}

\textbf{What we accomplished:} - {[}DONE{]} \textbf{G\_block}: Modular
generator block with TransposedConv + BatchNorm + ReLU - {[}DONE{]}
\textbf{D\_block}: Modular discriminator block with Conv + BatchNorm +
LeakyReLU - {[}DONE{]} \textbf{PokemonGenerator}: Complete 64x64 RGB
image generator - Progressive upsampling: 1x1 → 4x4 → 8x8 → 16x16 →
32x32 → 64x64 - Channel reduction: 512 → 256 → 128 → 64 → 3 (RGB)

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  {[}DONE{]} \textbf{PokemonDiscriminator}: Complete image classifier

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Progressive downsampling: 64x64 → 32x32 → 16x16 → 8x8 → 4x4 → 1x1
  \item
    Channel expansion: 3 (RGB) → 64 → 128 → 256 → 512 → 1 (logit)
  \end{itemize}
\item
  {[}DONE{]} \textbf{DCGAN Weight Initialization}: Proper initialization
  for stable training
\item
  {[}DONE{]} \textbf{Training Loop}: Complete with real-time
  visualization
\end{itemize}

\textbf{Key Learning Points:}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \textbf{Modular Architecture}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Building blocks make architecture flexible and readable
  \item
    Easy to adjust depth by adding/removing blocks
  \item
    Generator and discriminator are symmetric (mirror images)
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{DCGAN Innovations}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    All-convolutional (no pooling/unpooling layers)
  \item
    Batch normalization in all layers (except input/output)
  \item
    ReLU in generator, LeakyReLU in discriminator
  \item
    Tanh output for generator (matches {[}-1, 1{]} normalized data)
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Architectural Relationships}:

\begin{verbatim}
Generator:     Noise → Expand channels → Upsample → Reduce channels → RGB
Discriminator: RGB → Expand channels → Downsample → Compress → Single value
\end{verbatim}
\item
  \textbf{Why LeakyReLU in Discriminator?}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Regular ReLU can ``die'' (always output 0)
  \item
    LeakyReLU allows small negative gradients (\(\alpha\)=0.2)
  \item
    Helps discriminator learn from negative features
  \item
    More stable training
  \end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Architecture Comparison:}

\begin{verbatim}
MNIST GAN:    28x28x1 grayscale, simpler architecture
Pokemon DCGAN: 64x64x3 RGB, deeper network, modular blocks
\end{verbatim}

\textbf{Next Step:} Load Pokemon dataset and execute training

Load the Pokemon dataset, create normalization function, initialize
DCGAN models, and execute training.

\begin{itemize}

  \textbf{Pokemon Dataset}: Collected Pokemon sprites from multiple
  generations
\item
  \textbf{Image Size}: 64x64 RGB
\item
  \textbf{Normalization}: Scale to {[}-1, 1{]} to match generator's tanh
  output
\end{itemize}

                \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+se}{\PYZbs{}r}\PY{l+s+s2}{[}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{o}{*}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n}{done}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{ }\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{o}{*}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{50}\PY{o}{\PYZhy{}}\PY{n}{done}\PY{p}{)}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{] }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{downloaded}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{/}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{total\PYZus{}size}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ bytes}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{end}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+se}{\PYZbs{}n}\PY{l+s+s2}{[OK] Download complete!}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf+fm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}init\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{root\PYZus{}dir}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{transform}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{None}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}

        \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{pokemon\PYZus{}id} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{os}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{listdir}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{pokemon\PYZus{}dir}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{n}{pokemon\PYZus{}folder} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{os}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{path}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{join}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{pokemon\PYZus{}dir}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{pokemon\PYZus{}id}\PY{p}{)}

                        \PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{image\PYZus{}paths}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{append}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{os}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{path}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{join}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{pokemon\PYZus{}folder}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{img\PYZus{}name}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{k}{def}\PY{+w}{ }\PY{n+nf+fm}{\PYZus{}\PYZus{}len\PYZus{}\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb+bp}{self}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Define transformations}

    \PY{n}{transforms}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{Resize}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{64}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{64}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{,}

\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Create dataset and dataloader}

\PY{n}{POKEMON\PYZus{}BATCH\PYZus{}SIZE} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{256}

    \PY{n}{drop\PYZus{}last}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Drop last incomplete batch for stable training}
\PY{p}{)}

\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Part 2b) Visualize Pokemon images}

\PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{i}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{ax} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{enumerate}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{axes}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{flat}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}

\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{tight\PYZus{}layout}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
\PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{show}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

\PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Weights initialized with DCGAN scheme}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}

    \subsubsection{{[}THINKING{]} Part 2f) Understanding the Training
Block}\label{thinking-part-2f-understanding-the-training-block}

\textbf{Assignment Task}: Explain your understanding about the training
block. What is your understanding from the graph?

\textbf{Explanation of the Training Process:}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Weight Initialization}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    All convolutional layers initialized with Normal(0, 0.02)
  \item
    Batch normalization weights: Normal(1, 0.02)
  \item
    Critical for preventing gradient issues in deep networks
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Training Loop}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{Discriminator Update}:

    \begin{itemize}
    \tightlist
    \item
      Processes real Pokemon images → should output approx1
    \item
      Processes generated (fake) images → should output approx0
    \item
      Backpropagates total loss to improve classification
    \end{itemize}
  \item
    \textbf{Generator Update}:

    \begin{itemize}
    \tightlist
    \item
      Generates fake Pokemon from random noise
    \item
      Discriminator evaluates them
    \item
      Generator learns to fool discriminator (wants output approx1)
    \item
      Only generator weights updated (discriminator frozen)
    \end{itemize}
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Optimization}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    Adam optimizer with \(\beta\)\(_1\)=0.5 (lower than typical 0.9)
  \item
    Helps with GAN training stability
  \item
    Learning rate: 0.0005
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Expected Behavior}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \item
    Early epochs: Blurry, color-blob images
  \item
    Middle epochs: Pokemon-like shapes emerge - The goal is stability,
    not necessarily low loss values
  \end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{Understanding from the Loss Graph:} - Late epochs: Recognizable
Pokemon features (eyes, limbs, patterns) - Oscillations are normal and
expected in GAN training

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Loss values alone don't indicate quality - must inspect generated
  images
\end{itemize}

The loss curves show the adversarial competition between generator and
discriminator: - The adversarial game reaches equilibrium when both
networks are equally matched

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\setcounter{enumi}{3}
\item
  \textbf{What the Graph Tells Us}:
\item
  \textbf{Discriminator Loss (Red)}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \item
    Represents how well D distinguishes real from fake - Visual quality
    improves even if losses fluctuate
  \item
    When D loss is low (approx0.5-1.0): D is correctly classifying most
    images - Losses stabilize over time (convergence)
  \item
    When D loss spikes: G has temporarily fooled D with better fakes -
    Neither network dominates (no collapse)
  \item
    Both losses oscillate but remain relatively balanced
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Generator Loss (Blue)}:3. \textbf{Healthy Training Pattern}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \item
    Represents how well G fools D
  \item
    When G loss is low: G successfully tricks D (fake images classified
    as real) - When G loss is high: D detects fakes easily, G needs
    improvement
  \end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

    \begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

summarize key learnings.

\textbf{Key Learning Achievements:}

\begin{enumerate}

\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{DCGAN Architecture}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{Modular design}: G\_block and D\_block for flexible
    architecture
  \item
    \textbf{All-convolutional}: No pooling or fully-connected hidden
    layers
  \item
    \textbf{Batch normalization}: Stabilizes training in deep networks
  \item
    \textbf{Proper activations}: ReLU in generator, LeakyReLU in
    discriminator
  \end{itemize}

  \textbf{Differences from Basic GAN (Part 1)}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{Scale}: 64x64 RGB vs 28x28 grayscale
  \item
    \textbf{Complexity}: Deeper network (4 blocks vs 2-3 layers)
  \item
    \textbf{Data}: More complex (Pokemon vs digits)
  \item
    \textbf{Training}: Requires more careful initialization and tuning
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Training Insights}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{Weight initialization critical}: Normal(0, 0.02) for
    stability
  \item
    \textbf{Beta1=0.5}: Lower momentum helps GAN convergence
  \item
    \textbf{Visualization essential}: Losses don't tell full story
  \item
    \textbf{Mode diversity}: Some generated samples better than others
  \end{itemize}
\item
  \textbf{Challenges Observed}:

  \begin{itemize}
  \tightlist
  \item
    \textbf{Mode collapse risk}: Generator might favor certain Pokemon
    styles
  \item
    \textbf{Artifacts}: Some blur and distortion in generated images
  \item
    \textbf{Training time}: Significantly longer than MNIST
  \item
    \textbf{Hyperparameter sensitivity}: Learning rate, architecture
    choices matter
  \end{itemize}
\end{enumerate}

\subsubsection{Real-World Applications:}\label{real-world-applications}

GANs are used for: - \textbf{Image Generation}: Art, design, synthetic
data - \textbf{Image-to-Image Translation}: Style transfer,
colorization, super-resolution - \textbf{Data Augmentation}: Creating
training data for other models - \textbf{Anomaly Detection}: Identifying
unusual patterns - \textbf{Drug Discovery}: Generating molecular
structures - \textbf{Fashion/Game Design}: Creating new designs and
characters

\subsubsection{What is DCGAN?}\label{what-is-dcgan}

\textbf{DCGAN = Deep Convolutional Generative Adversarial Network}

DCGAN introduced specific architectural guidelines that make GANs more
stable: 1. \textbf{Replace pooling with strided convolutions}
(discriminator) and transposed convolutions (generator) 2. \textbf{Use
batch normalization} in both generator and discriminator 3.
\textbf{Remove fully connected hidden layers} (all-convolutional
architecture) 4. \textbf{Use ReLU in generator} (except output layer
with Tanh) 5. \textbf{Use LeakyReLU in discriminator} (prevents dying
neurons)

These principles significantly improved GAN training stability compared
to earlier GANs!

\subsubsection{Ways to Improve Pokemon DCGAN
Results:}\label{ways-to-improve-pokemon-dcgan-results}

\textbf{1. Training Improvements (Easy to Implement):} - \textbf{More
epochs}: Increase from 40 to 100-200 epochs - \textbf{Learning rate
scheduling}: Reduce learning rate gradually
\texttt{python\ \ \ \ \ \ scheduler\_G\ =\ optim.lr\_scheduler.StepLR(optimizerG,\ step\_size=50,\ gamma=0.5)\ \ \ \ \ \ scheduler\_D\ =\ optim.lr\_scheduler.StepLR(optimizerD,\ step\_size=50,\ gamma=0.5)}
- \textbf{Label smoothing}: Use 0.9 instead of 1.0 for real labels
(reduces discriminator overconfidence) - \textbf{Different learning
rates}: Try lr\_D=0.0004, lr\_G=0.0001 (slower discriminator)

\textbf{2. Architecture Improvements (Moderate):} - \textbf{Spectral
normalization}: Stabilizes discriminator training -
\textbf{Self-attention layers}: Better captures long-range dependencies
- \textbf{Deeper networks}: Add more G\_block/D\_block layers -
\textbf{Residual connections}: Skip connections for gradient flow

\textbf{3. Advanced Techniques (More Complex):} - \textbf{Progressive
Growing}: Start with 8x8, gradually increase to 64x64 -
\textbf{Wasserstein GAN (WGAN-GP)}: Better loss function, more stable -
\textbf{StyleGAN architecture}: State-of-the-art for high-quality images
- \textbf{Conditional GAN}: Control Pokemon type/color during generation
- \textbf{Data augmentation}: Flip, rotate, color jitter Pokemon images

\textbf{4. Dataset Improvements:} - \textbf{More data}: Collect
additional Pokemon images - \textbf{Better preprocessing}: Remove
backgrounds, center Pokemon - \textbf{Consistent sizing}: Ensure all
images are properly scaled

\textbf{Quick Win to Try Now:}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# In the training cell, change NUM\_EPOCHS\_POKEMON from 40 to 100}
\NormalTok{NUM\_EPOCHS\_POKEMON }\OperatorTok{=} \DecValTok{100}

\CommentTok{\# And modify learning rates}
\NormalTok{LEARNING\_RATE\_POKEMON }\OperatorTok{=} \FloatTok{0.0002}  \CommentTok{\# Reduce from 0.0005}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\textbf{Expected Improvement:} With 100 epochs and lower learning rate,
you should see: - Sharper edges on generated Pokemon - More consistent
color schemes - Better defined features (eyes, limbs) - Less color
bleeding and artifacts

\textbf{Remember:} Even with improvements, Pokemon generation is harder
than MNIST. The results will never be photo-realistic with basic DCGAN,
but they should show clear Pokemon-like characteristics!

\subsubsection{Final Thoughts:}\label{final-thoughts}

This assignment demonstrated that GANs can learn complex data
distributions without explicit supervision. The generator never sees
real images directly---it only receives gradients from the
discriminator. This adversarial process creates a powerful learning
paradigm that has revolutionized generative modeling in computer vision.

\textbf{The journey from random noise to realistic images showcases the
power of deep learning and adversarial training!}
